## Surveys

| Date | Resource | Environments | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2025‑03‑30 | [A Survey of WebAgents: Towards Next-Generation AI Agents for Web Automation with Large Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23350) | web | Surveys foundation-model web agents through their architectures, training methods and trustworthiness. It organizes research on completing browser tasks from user instructions and identifies directions for further development. |
| 2024‑11‑17 | [Generalist Virtual Agents: A Survey on Autonomous Agents Across Digital Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10943) | web, desktop, android, windows | Surveys virtual agents for browsers, applications and operating systems, organizing prior work by task type, observation and action spaces, model strategies and evaluation methods. It examines screen-based interaction and keyboard, mouse and touch control, alongside transferability and long-sequence decision-making challenges. |

## Papers

| Date | Resource | Environments | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2026‑11‑04 | [Securing Web Agents with the Compile-Then-Execute Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06039) | web | Baya compiles a web task into a program using a Trusted Website API before viewing live content, limiting how webpage prompt injections can alter agent control flow. |
| 2026‑11‑03 | [JHiMA-WebRL: A Joint Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05263) | web | Introduces JHiMA-WebRL, which jointly trains a task decomposer and GUI actor with hierarchical RL for long-horizon web tasks in WebArena. |
| 2026‑11‑03 | [Voluntary Servitude: Weaponized Safety Warning Mechanisms for Controlling AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04393) | web | Studies fabricated hazard warnings in reviews that steer agents’ choices without explicit commands, including transfer to browser agents, and tests a corroboration requirement. |
| 2026‑11‑02 | [From Local Skills to Long-Horizon Web Tasks: Progressive Skill Exploration for LLM Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03116) | web | Introduces SCOPE, a training method for long web-agent tasks that discovers local skills from rollouts, composes them into chains, and expands them using failure experience. |
| 2026‑11‑02 | [WebHarbor: Docking Real Websites for Evolving GUI Agent Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03634) | web | Introduces WebHarbor, a suite of 153 executable website environments and 3,518 tasks for evaluating and training GUI web agents on interactive workflows. |
| 2026‑10‑31 | [When 200 OK Is Not an Answer: Silent Fetch Failures in Agentic Web Content Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00884) | web | Studies silent failures in web content fetched by static and rendered-browser clients, and evaluates checks for whether HTTP 200 pages contain usable content. |
| 2026‑10‑30 | [AgentShield: Fortifying Web Applications Against AI Agents and Agentic Browsers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41181) | web | Evaluates agentic browsers against web anti-bot defenses and introduces AgentShield, a behavioral fingerprinting framework for distinguishing agents from people. |
| 2026‑10‑30 | [ANGLER: Verifier-Grounded Benchmarking with an Adaptive Attacker for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41272) | web | Introduces REEF, a website benchmark with state-based attack verification, and ANGLER, which adapts attack lures using outcomes from web-agent rollouts. |
| 2026‑10‑30 | [PatchScout: Thematic Web Data Collection via Information Foraging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00169) | web | Introduces PatchScout, a multi-agent method for collecting thematic documents from the live web by deciding when to explore a new information region. |
| 2026‑10‑29 | [AgentHeal: Evaluating Goal-Preserving Resource Recovery of Web Agents under Access Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39234) | web | Introduces AgentHeal, a benchmark of whether web agents detect access barriers, find legitimate alternative sources, and preserve the user's original goal. |
| 2026‑10‑29 | [DynaRev: Benchmarking Web Agents under Dynamic Goal Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40133) | web | Introduces DynaRev, a benchmark of 181 web-agent tasks whose goals change during execution, with trajectory-based measures of correctness, responsiveness, and efficiency. |
| 2026‑10‑29 | [InterA11y: Interactive Web Accessibility Error Detection with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39488) | web | Introduces InterA11y, a web accessibility evaluation system that guides LLM agents with rubrics and tools for inspecting and operating pages. |
| 2026‑10‑27 | [Right Click, Wrong Box: Grading GUI Grounding by What a Click Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36831) | web | Proposes grading GUI grounding clicks by their executed outcomes and audits box-based scores on live web applications and ScreenSpot-v2 screenshots. |
| 2026‑10‑27 | [WebAnything: From the Wild Web to Scalable Agent Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37786) | web | Introduces WebAnything, a framework that reconstructs reusable, resettable website environments from real URLs for web-agent training and evaluation. |
| 2026‑10‑26 | [Back to Basics: A Skill-Centric Approach to Generalist Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35823) | web | Introduces MiniWeb for skill-labeled browser tasks, MiniWebGen for generating training trajectories, and MiniWebAgent for composing learned skills into web workflows. |
| 2026‑10‑26 | [Harness-Model Collaboration for Long-Horizon Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34724) | web | Introduces HaMo, a continual-learning framework for web agents that retrieves strategy cards from past workflows and updates model parameters to reuse them. |
| 2026‑10‑25 | [AgentProofBench: Benchmarking Reusable Verifiers for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34634) | web | Introduces AgentProofBench, a benchmark linking tests of reusable web-agent verification rules to subsequent execution from saved web states. |
| 2026‑10‑24 | [SkillShift-Bench: Benchmarking Skill Learning under Environment Evolution for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33528) | web | Introduces SkillShift-Bench to evaluate how web agents reuse learned skills when task context, visual perception, or execution conditions change across web systems. |
| 2026‑10‑19 | [Deep Search in Real-World Web Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30181) | web | OpenDS-World benchmarks multi-step research on the live open Web, requiring agents to search, interact with browsers and combine evidence across sources. |
| 2026‑10‑19 | [In-Context Teacher–Student Guidance for Open-Weight Browser Agents](https://sol.sbc.org.br/index.php/kdmile/article/view/44978) | web | Training-free protocol that turns a teacher model's offline reasoning traces into prompt guidance for open-weight browser agents; on MiniWoB++ it lifts solved student-task pairs from 208 to 271 across ten students. |
| 2026‑10‑16 | [PersonaCUABench: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agents Serving Diverse Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22990) | web | PersonaCUABench varies simulated user requests and clarification dialogue over fixed web tasks, comparing task completion with requirement handling and persona-agent satisfaction ratings. |
| 2026‑10‑16 | [SPIDES: Information-Flow Control for Browser Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24277) | web | SPIDES propagates HTML integrity and confidentiality labels into agent context and mediates browser actions with information-flow policies and approval gates, evaluated in the labeled MicroNet ecosystem. |
| 2026‑10‑15 | [Don't Retrain, Remix: Adaptation of Web Agents using Mixture Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22539) | web | WebMix uses a scout-produced interface manual to route mixtures of source-trained LoRA adapters for web agents, adapting to target interfaces without target-time gradient updates. |
| 2026‑10‑15 | [What It Is, Not What It Says: Securing Agentic Browsing Interactions with Web Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22697) | web | Intact records webpage element provenance and capabilities, isolates planning per element, and uses a browser harness to constrain actions and request consent for third-party interactions. |
| 2026‑10‑14 | [Teaching Agents in their Own Style: Blackbox Teacher Distillation for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19834) | web | Introduces WebKD, which repairs failed student web-agent trajectories with minimal teacher edits and trains on corrections that preserve the student's reasoning style. |
| 2026‑10‑14 | [Why Generate a Click? Plug-in Decision Heads for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20107) | web | Adds decision heads to a frozen vision-language generator for selecting web actions and targets, with a confidence gate that falls back to text generation. |
| 2026‑10‑13 | [Reading Right, Acting Wrong: Diagnosing Decision Failures of System One Models in Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18579) | web | Pairs factual probes with next-action choices on frozen agent traces, including Mind2Web, to diagnose reading-decision gaps without closed-loop execution. |
| 2026‑10‑12 | [BrowseSafe++: Benchmarking Prompt Injection Detection Across Web Contexts, Languages, and Browser Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17457) | web | Extends BrowseSafe with paired attacked and clean webpages in several representations, and studies text and screenshot detectors plus feedback-adapted prompt injections. |
| 2026‑10‑12 | [Glasseek: A Transparent Recipe for Training and Trustworthy Evaluation of DeepSearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16998) | web | Introduces Glasseek, a framework for deep-search agents that explore the open web, with trajectory construction, training across context compression, and a test-set leakage audit. |
| 2026‑10‑12 | [HyWe: Unifying Search and Operation for Real-World Hybrid Web Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16319) | web | Introduces HyWe, which switches between information search and browser operation within one trajectory, alongside a demonstration pipeline and a benchmark of hybrid web tasks. |
| 2026‑10‑09 | [A Baseline Assessment for the Agentic Web Technology Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13703) | web | Presents MASTIF, a framework for comparing combinations of agent models, protocols, and implementation frameworks on web tasks, with an initial assessment using 300 Mind2Web tasks. |
| 2026‑10‑09 | [Web$hell: A Unified Interface for Agentic Web Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13416) | web | Introduces Web$hell, an interface that compiles live pages into persistent information and action objects so agents can gather evidence, navigate websites, and reuse acquired content through shared operations. |
| 2026‑10‑08 | [Can LLMs Fix It Without Code? Toward Automated Verification of No-Code Bug Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11963) | web, desktop, linux | Tests natural-language no-code fixes through GUI execution in Linux Brave. Scripted fixed/buggy-state gates and issue-specific checkers distinguish fix outcomes from the executor's completion claims. |
| 2026‑10‑07 | [Secure-CUA: Controlling Untrusted Influence in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09469) | web | Proposes Secure-CUA, which commits to per-action transactions before reading untrusted page content and separates content queries from GUI target selection. The paper formalizes its protection assumptions and evaluates benign task utility on WebArena. |
| 2026‑10‑05 | [CLIFT: Conformal Self-Verification for Web Agent Training and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06829) | web | Introduces CLIFT, which certifies agent-generated verification signals against training-time judges and reuses them for per-step rewards and trajectory selection. At inference, a frozen question bank helps compare candidate web rollouts without querying an external judge. |
| 2026‑10‑02 | [HyperBrowseComp: A Multilingual and Multimodal Stress Test for Web-Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03574) | web | Introduces HyperBrowseComp, a multilingual benchmark for browsing agents that must connect evidence across open-web sources, including videos, scans, images, and maps. |
| 2026‑10‑02 | [WebFovea: When the Model Is Right but the Click Is Wrong -- Reliable Round Trips for Vision-Based Web Agents on Live Websites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03036) | web | Describes WebFovea, a vision-based live-web agent whose execution loop checks action parsing, browser effects, feedback accuracy, and observation delivery. The report analyzes failures such as coordinate mismatches and silent control interactions and documents corresponding harness changes. |
| 2026‑10‑02 | [WebUIProof: Benchmarking WebUI Code Generators with UI-Agent Execution Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02617) | web | Introduces an execution-based benchmark for generated web interfaces, with a headless-browser UI-agent harness that checks DOM interactions and functional assertions. |
| 2026‑10‑01 | [How To Train Your World Model: Fine-tuning vs RAG for LM-based World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02542) | web | Compares fine-tuned, retrieval-based and hybrid language-model world models for next-observation prediction and action selection in text environments including WebShop. |
| 2026‑09‑30 | [Action Conditioned Bisimulation For GUI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38778) | web | Defines a GUI-memory merge rule that treats two states as equivalent only when shared actions lead to matching outcomes and successor states. The training-free method replaces observation-similarity merging and is tested in closed-loop MiniWoB++ interaction. |
| 2026‑09‑29 | [AdaptArena: Evaluating Test-Time Personalization of Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36488) | web | Introduces AdaptArena to test whether web agents can infer unstated user preferences from relevant past trajectories and carry those preferences into new tasks. Its AdaptiveAgent baseline separates preference-retrieval challenges from failures in downstream web execution. |
| 2026‑09‑28 | [Structured Interaction, Visual Localization, and Robust Execution for Complex Web Tasks: A Technical Report on the WebRetriever Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35904) | web | Describes a WebRetriever agent that combines semantic webpage operations with visual localization when structured representations are insufficient. Hierarchical context management and fault-aware execution support processing tasks across concurrent browsers. |

| 2026‑03‑05 | [WebFactory: Automated Compression of Foundational Language Intelligence into Grounded Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05044) | web | Introduces WebFactory, a GUI-agent training pipeline that synthesizes websites, generates tasks and trajectories, and trains with decomposed rewards. | 2026‑02‑28 | [Atomicity for Agents: Exposing, Exploiting, and Mitigating TOCTOU Vulnerabilities in Browser-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00476) | web | Identifies time-of-check to time-of-use (TOCTOU) race vulnerabilities when web pages mutate between agent planning and action execution. |
| 2026‑02‑24 | [ActionEngine: From Reactive to Programmatic Web Agents via State Machine Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20502) | web | ActionEngine explores web applications to build an updateable state-machine memory of GUI states, operations and transitions. | 2026‑02‑19 | [Modeling Distinct Human Interaction in Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17588) | web | CowCorpus records interleaved human and agent actions during web navigation and identifies distinct patterns of user intervention. | 2026‑02‑19 | [Persona2Web: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents for Contextual Reasoning with User History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17003) | web | Persona2Web tests whether web agents can resolve ambiguous requests using preferences implicit in a user’s history. | 2026‑02‑19 | [Web Agents Should Use Typed Actions Instead of Click-Based Browsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17245) | web | Proposes web verbs as typed actions for web agents, exposing operations with structured inputs, outputs and conditions so agents can compose auditable workflows. |
| 2026‑02‑17 | [World-Model-Augmented Web Agents with Action Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15384) | web | WAC has a web-agent action model consult a world model, simulate action outcomes and use judge feedback to correct actions before execution. |
| 2026‑02‑16 | [EmbeWebAgent: Embedding Web Agents into Any Customized UI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14865) | web | EmbeWebAgent embeds agent access into existing web applications through frontend observations and a page-specific function registry. | 2026‑02‑15 | [AutoWebWorld: Synthesizing Infinite Verifiable Web Environments via Finite State Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14296) | web | AutoWebWorld turns finite-state task models into interactive websites whose actions and goal states can be checked programmatically. | 2026‑02‑14 | [OpAgent: Operator Agent for Web Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13559) | web | OpAgent combines multi-task GUI fine-tuning with online web reinforcement learning using outcome judgments and rule-based progress rewards. | 2026‑02‑13 | [Scaling Web Agent Training through Automatic Data Generation and Fine-grained Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12544) | web | This work uses constraint-based progress evaluation to retain partially successful web trajectories for training. | 2026‑02‑13 | [SPILLage: Agentic Oversharing on the Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13516) | web | Formalizes and measures natural agentic oversharing when browser agents handle private user emails and calendars across live websites. |
| 2026‑02‑12 | [Agentic Test-Time Scaling for WebAgents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12276) | web | CATTS allocates additional inference computation when an agent’s action votes show uncertainty. The paper compares voting and arbiter strategies and evaluates this selective scaling on WebArena-Lite, Online-Mind2Web and GoBrowse. |
| 2026‑02‑11 | [ICA: Information-Aware Credit Assignment for Visually Grounded Long-Horizon Information-Seeking Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10863) | web | Information-Aware Credit Assignment scores intermediate information gathered by web agents, using rendered webpage snapshots to compare evidence across trajectories. |
| 2026‑02‑09 | [MUZZLE: Adaptive Agentic Red-Teaming of Web Agents Against Indirect Prompt Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09222) | web | MUZZLE evaluates web-agent exposure to indirect prompt injection by finding attack surfaces in agent trajectories and refining injected instructions from execution feedback. |
| 2026‑02‑05 | [Do Web Agents Investigate Before They Decide?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05354) | web | Introduces MIRAGE, a web decision benchmark where agents must actively uncover evidence that can contradict the visible context. | 2026‑02‑03 | [WebSentinel: Detecting and Localizing Prompt Injection Attacks for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03792) | web | WebSentinel identifies suspicious webpage segments and checks each against its surrounding page context to detect and locate prompt injections. Its evaluation uses both contaminated and clean webpage datasets. |
| 2026‑02‑02 | [Avenir-Web: Human-Experience-Imitating Multimodal Web Agents with Mixture of Grounding Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02468) | web | Avenir-Web combines multiple grounding specialists with procedural experience, a task checklist and adaptive memory. | 2026‑02‑01 | [SimGym: Traffic-Grounded Browser Agents for Offline A/B Testing in E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01443) | web | SimGym runs traffic-grounded synthetic buyers as browser agents to compare e-commerce storefront variants offline, using buyer profiles derived from production interactions. |
| 2026‑01‑29 | [DynaWeb: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning of Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22149) | web | DynaWeb learns a world model that predicts web-page representations after actions and uses it to generate policy-training rollouts. It interleaves these rollouts with expert trajectories and evaluates the trained web agents on WebArena and WebVoyager. |
| 2026‑01‑29 | [How do Visual Attributes Influence Web Agents? A Comprehensive Evaluation of User Interface Design Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21961) | web | Introduces VAF, a controlled pipeline that varies webpage appearance while retaining item semantics. It measures both agent clicks and reasoning references to study which visual design factors change browsing decisions. |
| 2026‑01‑29 | [WebArbiter: A Principle-Guided Reasoning Process Reward Model for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21872) | web | WebArbiter generates reasoned action-preference verdicts for web navigation, using reasoning distillation followed by reinforcement learning. | 2026‑01‑26 | [MalURLBench: A Benchmark Evaluating Agents' Vulnerabilities When Processing Web URLs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18113) | web | MalURLBench evaluates how LLM-based web agents handle disguised malicious URLs across attack scenarios; the paper also analyzes attack factors and proposes URLGuard. |
| 2026‑01‑13 | [ExpSeek: Self-Triggered Experience Seeking for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08605) | web | ExpSeek uses step-level entropy signals to decide when a web agent should request experience-based guidance. | 2026‑01‑13 | [WebTrap Park: An Automated Platform for Systematic Security Evaluation of Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08406) | web | Introduces WebTrap Park, a platform that turns web-agent security risks into executable tasks and assesses concrete interactions with live pages. It compares agent frameworks without requiring modifications to their implementations. |
| 2026‑01‑12 | [ColorBrowserAgent: Complex Long-Horizon Browser Agent with Adaptive Knowledge Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07262) | web | ColorBrowserAgent converts human feedback into reusable website knowledge and uses that knowledge to guide progressive context summaries. | 2026‑01‑12 | [Web Agent Agentic Reinforcement Learning Decision Model Under Multi-Cost and Failure Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23857) | web | Proposes constrained reinforcement learning for web agents that weighs request costs, latency and failure risk across multi-step website tasks. |
| 2026‑01‑12 | [When Bots Take the Bait: Exposing and Mitigating the Emerging Social Engineering Attack in Web Automation Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07263) | web | Studies deceptive social-engineering web patterns targeting browser automation agents and proposes defenses against bait-based manipulation. |
| 2026‑01‑05 | [WebGym: Scaling Training Environments for Visual Web Agents with Realistic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02439) | web | WebGym supplies realistic web tasks with rubric-based evaluation and an asynchronous rollout system for reinforcement learning. | 2025‑12‑29 | [Nested Browser-Use Learning for Agentic Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23647) | web | NestBrowse organizes browser actions in a nested framework that separates interaction control from page exploration. It evaluates this design for deep information seeking beyond snippet retrieval and direct URL fetching. |
| 2025‑12‑28 | [DECEPTICON: How Dark Patterns Manipulate Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22894) | web | DECEPTICON is an environment of 700 web navigation tasks that isolates dark patterns to measure how deceptive interface designs steer agent actions. |
| 2025‑12‑14 | [ceLLMate: Sandboxing Browser AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12594) | web | ceLLMate constrains browser-agent authority through an extension that applies policies at the HTTP layer. | 2025‑12‑14 | [WebOperator: Action-Aware Tree Search for Autonomous Agents in Web Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12692) | web | WebOperator uses tree search, action filtering, and path checks before replay to explore web tasks and backtrack while accounting for irreversible actions. |
| 2025‑12‑08 | [Privacy Practices of Browser Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07725) | web | Presents a privacy evaluation framework for browser agents covering component weaknesses, website defenses, tracking, privacy-related prompts and personal-data leakage. | 2025‑12‑07 | [Permission Manifests for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02371) | web | Proposes agent-permissions.json, a website manifest for declaring which interactions web agents may perform, with optional references to APIs. |
| 2025‑12‑05 | [FronTalk: Benchmarking Front-End Development as Conversational Code Generation with Multi-Modal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04203) | web | FronTalk benchmarks multi-turn front-end code generation from text and visual feedback; a web agent explores generated sites for evaluation and in AceCoder. |
| 2025‑11‑28 | [LegalWebAgent: Empowering Access to Justice via LLM-Based Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04105) | web | LegalWebAgent separates user-needs clarification, webpage browsing, and actions such as form completion or scheduling. | 2025‑11‑28 | [MCP vs RAG vs NLWeb vs HTML: A Comparison of the Effectiveness and Efficiency of Different Agent Interfaces to the Web (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23281) | web | Compares HTML browsing, RAG, MCP, and NLWeb interfaces for web agents performing product search, comparison, and checkout tasks in four simulated e-shops. |
| 2025‑11‑27 | [Real-Time Procedural Learning From Experience for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22074) | web | PRAXIS stores action outcomes and retrieves past state-action-result examples to guide agent decisions, with evaluation on the REAL web browsing benchmark. |
| 2025‑11‑25 | [BrowseSafe: Understanding and Preventing Prompt Injection Within AI Browser Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20597) | web | BrowseSafe builds a benchmark of prompt injections in realistic HTML for browser agents and evaluates defenses against attacks that can affect agent actions. |
| 2025‑11‑24 | [Fara-7B: An Efficient Agentic Model for Computer Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19663) | web | Introduces FaraGen synthetic web trajectories, a compact screenshot-based action model, and WebTailBench for underrepresented browser tasks. |
| 2025‑11‑22 | [Building Browser Agents: Architecture, Security, and Practical Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19477) | web | Reports architectural and security lessons from building a browser agent, including hybrid accessibility-tree and visual context, browser tooling and programmatic constraints. | 2025‑11‑17 | [WebCoach: Self-Evolving Web Agents with Cross-Session Memory Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12997) | web | Introduces WebCoach, which condenses navigation logs into persistent episodic memory and retrieves experiences by similarity and recency. Runtime hooks selectively supply advice to browser agents, evaluated on WebVoyager without retraining the underlying models. |
| 2025‑11‑11 | [AgentPRM: Process Reward Models for LLM Agents via Step-Wise Promise and Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08325) | web | AgentPRM scores sequential agent decisions by their promise and progress toward a goal, with web shopping and browser navigation among the motivating tasks. |
| 2025‑11‑09 | [WebVIA: A Web-based Vision-Language Agentic Framework for Interactive and Verifiable UI-to-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06251) | web | WebVIA combines a web UI exploration agent, interactive UI-to-Code generation, and validation of generated code's interactivity. |
| 2025‑11‑08 | [SynthAgent: Adapting Web Agents with Synthetic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06101) | web | Introduces SynthAgent, which generates tasks by exploring website elements and refines them when observations reveal conflicts. | 2025‑10‑26 | [WebATLAS: An LLM Agent with Experience-Driven Memory and Action Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22732) | web | WebATLAS is a web agent that stores interaction outcomes and simulates candidate actions before acting, using past experience to plan navigation on unseen websites. |
| 2025‑10‑18 | [Branch-and-Browse: Efficient and Controllable Web Exploration with Tree-Structured Reasoning and Action Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19838) | web | Branch-and-Browse is a web agent framework that uses tree-structured subtasks, web state replay, and page action memory to explore and complete tasks. |
| 2025‑10‑17 | [PolySkill: Learning Generalizable Skills Through Polymorphic Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15863) | web | Introduces PolySkill, which separates a web-agent skill's goal from its execution so agents can learn and reuse skills across websites. |
| 2025‑10‑17 | [WEBSERV: A Full-Stack and RL-Ready Web Environment for Training Web Agents at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16252) | web | WebServ combines copy-on-write container environments with a compact DOM-derived browser interface for training web agents. | 2025‑10‑16 | [WebAggregator: Enhancing Compositional Reasoning Capabilities of Deep Research Agent Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14438) | web | Introduces WebAggregator, a pipeline for synthesizing compositional questions from websites to train deep-research agents, plus the WebAggregatorQA testbed. |
| 2025‑10‑14 | [HackWorld: Evaluating Computer-Use Agents on Exploiting Web Application Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12200) | web | Introduces HackWorld for evaluating visual computer-use agents in a capture-the-flag collection of web applications. | 2025‑10‑13 | [WebRouter: Query-specific Router via Variational Information Bottleneck for Cost-sensitive Web Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11221) | web | Introduces WebRouter, a cost-aware router that compresses web-agent prompts to choose among LLMs, with cost and accuracy measured on WebVoyager websites. |
| 2025‑10‑12 | [BrowserAgent: Building Web Agents with Human-Inspired Web Browsing Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10666) | web | Trains BrowserAgent to use Playwright-based scrolling, clicking and typing directly on webpages for information-seeking tasks. | 2025‑10‑10 | [WARC-Bench: Web Archive Based Benchmark for GUI Subtask Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09872) | web | WARC-Bench packages dynamic web pages as replayable archives and evaluates short navigation subtasks involving components such as date pickers and scrollable containers. | 2025‑10‑05 | [JEF-Hinter: Leveraging Offline Knowledge for Improving Web Agents Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04373) | web | Introduces JEF-Hinter, which distills successful and failed trajectories into compact episodic hints and retrieves them for the current agent state. | 2025‑10‑04 | [Cross-Modal Content Optimization for Steering Web Agent Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03612) | web | Introduces Cross-Modal Preference Steering (CPS), a black-box attack that edits a listing's images and text to steer VLM-based web agents' selections. |
| 2025‑10‑02 | [BrowserArena: Evaluating LLM Agents on Real-World Web Navigation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02418) | web | Introduces BrowserArena, a live-web platform for head-to-head agent comparisons on user-submitted tasks. | 2025‑10‑01 | [Graph2Eval: Automatic Multimodal Task Generation for Agents via Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00507) | web | Introduces Graph2Eval, which generates multimodal agent evaluation tasks from knowledge graphs, including multi-step web interactions in Graph2Eval-Bench. |
| 2025‑10‑01 | [WALT: Web Agents that Learn Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01524) | web | WALT explores website functionality and converts demonstrated interactions into validated, reusable tools combining DOM actions, URL navigation, and agentic fallbacks. | 2025‑09‑29 | [ReasoningBank: Scaling Agent Self-Evolving with Reasoning Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25140) | web | Distills generalizable reasoning strategies from both successful and failed agent trajectories into a persistent memory bank for test-time retrieval. |
| 2025‑09‑29 | [Retrieval-augmented GUI Agents with Generative Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24183) | web, android | RAG-GUI trains a small guidance adapter to filter and summarize retrieved web tutorials while keeping the acting agent fixed. | 2025‑09‑28 | [WAREX: Web Agent Reliability Evaluation on Existing Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03285) | web | WAREX augments existing web-agent benchmarks with reliability challenges arising from unstable systems and changing or disruptive website behavior. | 2025‑09‑26 | [Can We Stop Malicious AI? | 2025‑09‑25 | [Recon-Act: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Browser-Use System via Web Reconnaissance, Tool Generation, and Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21072) | web | Introduces Recon-Act, a browser-use framework that compares failed and successful web trajectories to create reusable tools for planning and executing long-horizon website tasks. |
| 2025‑09‑17 | [Detecting Pipeline Failures through Fine-Grained Analysis of Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14382) | web | Introduces a modular framework for identifying errors at intermediate stages of a web-agent pipeline. | 2025‑09‑17 | [TGPO: Tree-Guided Preference Optimization for Robust Web Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14172) | web | TGPO merges semantically equivalent web states into tree-structured trajectories for offline preference optimization. | 2025‑09‑16 | [ReSum: Unlocking Long-Horizon Search Intelligence via Context Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13313) | web | Introduces ReSum, which compresses interaction histories during long web searches, and ReSum-GRPO, which trains agents to reason across the resulting segments. |
| 2025‑09‑15 | [PrivWeb: Unobtrusive and Content-aware Privacy Protection For Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11939) | web | Introduces PrivWeb, which uses a local language model to anonymize interface information according to user preferences. | 2025‑08‑29 | [MMSearch-Plus: Benchmarking Provenance-Aware Search for Multimodal Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21475) | web | Introduces MMSearch-Plus, a multimodal browsing benchmark with iterative image-text search, and an agent framework that marks and crops images for targeted retrieval. |
| 2025‑08‑29 | [Morae: Proactively Pausing UI Agents for User Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21456) | web | Introduces Morae, a UI agent that detects decision points and pauses for user choices using interface code and screenshots. | 2025‑08‑23 | [WebSight: A Vision-First Architecture for Robust Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16987) | web | WebSight uses a vision-language model trained on web-interface examples within a modular agent with planning, action, verification, and episodic memory components. | 2025‑08‑18 | [A Functionality-Grounded Benchmark for Evaluating Web Agents in E-commerce Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15832) | web | Amazon-Bench generates functionality-specific web tasks from page content and interactive controls, covering operations beyond product search. Its evaluator checks task completion and unintended changes to account state, such as address or purchase settings. |
| 2025‑08‑18 | [WebMall -- A Multi-Shop Benchmark for Evaluating Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13024) | web | WebMall provides four simulated online shops with heterogeneous product data for comparison shopping, related-product searches, and checkout tasks. It evaluates web agents with different observation formats and memory configurations in a fixed offline environment. |
| 2025‑08‑14 | [MM-BrowseComp: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13186) | web | Introduces MM-BrowseComp, a benchmark for browsing agents that must use evidence from web images and videos to answer multimodal search questions. |
| 2025‑08‑07 | [Cognitive Duality for Adaptive Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05081) | web | Introduces CogniWeb, which switches between fast reactive processing and slower deliberative reasoning according to task complexity. WebArena experiments examine this combination of learned behaviors and online planning. |
| 2025‑08‑06 | [Beyond Pixels: Exploring DOM Downsampling for LLM-Based Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04412) | web | D2Snap reduces serialized DOM observations while preserving actionable elements and features that distinguish their uses. | 2025‑08‑06 | [HarmonyGuard: Toward Safety and Utility in Web Agents via Adaptive Policy Enhancement and Dual-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04010) | web | Introduces HarmonyGuard, a multi-agent framework that updates security policies and weighs safety against task completion for agents operating on the open web. |
| 2025‑08‑03 | [Web-CogReasoner: Towards Multimodal Knowledge-Induced Cognitive Reasoning for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01858) | web | Web-CogReasoner separates factual and conceptual web knowledge from procedural reasoning, using a structured curriculum to train a multimodal agent. Web-CogDataset and Web-CogBench support study of these capabilities alongside web-navigation evaluation. |
| 2025‑07‑31 | [General Agentic Planning Through Simulative Reasoning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23773) | web | Introduces SiRA, an agent architecture that uses a language-based world model to simulate action outcomes for planning in web-browser tasks. |
| 2025‑07‑30 | [Magentic-UI: Towards Human-in-the-loop Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22358) | web | Magentic-UI combines browser interaction, code execution, file operations, and MCP tools in an interface for human-agent collaboration. It studies collaborative planning and task execution, action guards, and persistent memory through benchmark tasks, simulated users, user studies, and safety assessments. |
| 2025‑07‑20 | [Manipulating LLM Web Agents with Indirect Prompt Injection Attack via HTML Accessibility Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14799) | web | Studies indirect prompt injection through HTML content consumed as accessibility trees. The evaluation tests how manipulated page observations redirect browser-agent actions on real websites. |
| 2025‑07‑15 | [Let's Think in Two Steps: Mitigating Agreement Bias in MLLMs with Self-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11662) | web | Studies agreement bias in multimodal agent verifiers across web navigation and computer use, and introduces SGV to assess trajectories using self-generated priors. |
| 2025‑07‑08 | [R-VLM: Region-Aware Vision Language Model for Precise GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05673) | web, cross-platform | R-VLM localizes GUI elements through zoomed-in region proposals and an intersection-over-union-aware training objective. It evaluates the grounding model on ScreenSpot and AgentStudio and studies its effect on navigation in AITW and Mind2Web. |
| 2025‑07‑06 | [WebSynthesis: World-Model-Guided MCTS for Efficient WebUI-Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04370) | web | Introduces WebSynthesis, which uses a learned web world model and tree-based planning to synthesize interaction trajectories for training web agents. |
| 2025‑07‑04 | [Less is More: Empowering GUI Agent with Context-Aware Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03730) | web, cross-platform | SimpAgent prunes distracting interface elements with visual masks and compresses interaction history using consistency-based guidance.
